- scope_allowlist: src/server.ts、新src/relay-mix-pump.ts、新test/relay-mix-pump.test.ts；必要owner定位test/server-mic-capture-restart-coordinator.test.ts、test/server-audio-uplink-coordinator.test.ts、test/webtransport-media-server.test.ts、test/audio-session-mic-gain-authority.test.ts（level context第四處gain query搬移，須保留權威保障）。既有test/mixer-refactor.bench.ts可作test-only PERF；若需要pump専用mode/fixture helper，先補具體檔案與理由，不用原drain量測推論新scheduler零成本。
- symbol_move: 5ms mixer callback→具名tick；processPublisherFrame與deliverMicPackets→同pump；最後mixerTimer handle→pump顯式start/stop。先具名tick由舊server setInterval呼叫，再整合ingest，最後換scheduler，逐步記錄證據。public methods tick/deliver/ingest/start/stop具名同步、不用handle(any)或eventbus。
- state_kept: AudioSession clock/PCM/evidence/generation/limiter/readers全部原owner；MicRuntime auth/receiver/retransmits不變；Take append/writer/quality query不變；startLiveSource、resetMicAudibility、reportMicAudibility（receiver window baseline）、reportMicLevel、reportMicTimelineFolds（fold counter及timing/probe mutation）與robotContentFallbackPrimingActive policy留caller，以具名query/effect ports接入。captureRestartCoordinator原交易重用，不複製。pump唯一新增state為自己的interval handle，不cache sampleRate/flow/quality/clock。
- ports: grouped narrow Pick mix/mic/audibility/level/drift/calibration/validator/transition/take/monitor＋clock及具名lifecycle/report query/effect；無ServerContext、mutable bag、任意setter、constructor callback。methods保留receiver；sampleRate guard/ingest/drift各原時點獨立讀，不為省查詢cache。
- clock/coordinate/identity: monotonicnowA只供retransmits+flush；每ingest重新取clock；session.drain只傳syncemit保留自取clock/defaultmax5；每emittedframe重新取nowB；quality→append→monitor→audibility→level→fold完全原序。micPlayable同frame問兩次，level contextlazy並保持songPlaying→gain order。PCM/evidence/position同identity，不重編碼/補packet或修改accepted判定；samples.length=0不noteFrame但保留其餘舊meter/timingeffects。
- lifecycle: factory不得開timer；顯式start在原mixerTimer建構點，仍早於awaitOpus/awaitWT/HTTPlisten。wss.close與gracefulShutdown兩個clearInterval入口都接stop；shutdown先raise fence/stop混音→cancelgrace→awaitTake(Date.now)→WTstop→terminate+closeWSS→HTTPclose；不改signal/error/exitpolicy或把tickasync化。重複start/stop不創兩個timer，stop後scheduledcallback不能產生新frame，restart只啟一個新handle。
- C0 cases: 由舊server source adapter取得tick/ingest trace（不是手寫第二份production算法），同ports驗證無transport仍drain、多包flush按序、多frame獨立clock、zeroaccepted不ack、transport/rate guards及shortcircuit、driftobservefalse不estimate、nullgeneration不drift、capture restart cleanup-beforeconsumers、fallbackprimeguard、lazy levelquery、take/monitor sameframe identity、throws同停止點。scheduler cases在C1新增fake scheduler與production binding/start/stop/shutdown定位保障。

- baseline：已驗證 S11，worktree `/home/mochi/relay-wip/deep-refactor`／branch refactor/mic-timeline／HEAD `7b5d550682d43ae8eba3f55aeff3bf0b239ba056`，777-file fingerprint `819baf5758f787f69061e22501650a2a90d75aa6fe3cf6f6e4379fb88008edaa`，完整 source archive 沿 S11-frozen-source.tar。原11 dirty＋A/S10/S11成果保留。重新核對 worktree/HEAD/Node20.19.2/npm9.2.0、diff-check0；root/parents與src/test中未找到適用AGENTS。已重讀00/02/04、SESSION_MODEL、ARCHITECTURE_BOUNDARIES。
- 目前符號：server route/readiness/helper 群575–711；clear/revocation/rate/begin/reconcile/frame/request 群718–872；mapper/offset/transition/coordinators保留自身來源檔。所有production callers及 source-contract定位已rg核對，不按文件行號切割。
- scope_allowlist：`src/server.ts`、新增 `src/relay-robot-mapping-orchestration.ts`、新增 `test/relay-robot-mapping-orchestration.test.ts`；必要定位/接線更新限 `test/runtime-single-snapshot.test.ts`、`test/server-robot-content-evidence-readiness.test.ts`、`test/server-robot-content-anchor-readiness.test.ts`、`test/server-robot-bootstrap-timing-deadlock.test.ts`、`test/server-robot-content-mapping-revocation-coordinator.test.ts`、`test/server-robot-content-transition-teardown.test.ts`、`test/playback-rate-timing.test.ts`。其他定位檔如確有新failure，先明列必要性再修改。domain source／wire／policy／golden／timeouts不在白名單。

- 搬走caller的責任：probeGeneration/context/path readiness、request/admission、共同client identity fence、reply/failure組裝、analysis readiness→evidence→sync correlation→leg→combine→promotion、reapply事實取樣與promotion接線、abandon委派、兩個coordinator constructions。新factory `createRelayBootProbeOrchestration`提供context/stepAdmission/stepAnalysis/stepReapply/handleReply/handleFailure/failAttempt/abandon；群內helper private，只對仍有真caller的入口保留原server名稱薄wrapper（default參數若有仍保留）。全部caller含bootProbeSettled與calibration queries經同context入口，不留下第二份算法。
- 刻意留下：原domain state與request sequence/retry/attempt/context/result authority、同步DSP算法/原cache、CalibrationSession的async worker與settlement、manual restart transaction/command admission、clearBootCalibrationState完整reset、bootProbeSettled策略後繼query、currentDeltaMs/bootProbeAdvanceMs共享queries、250ms timer/step5 expiry與8/9/10順序、protocol handlers/sendJson與payload projection。不是新增boot worker、eventbus、timer、async queue或大ServerContext。
- 依賴設計：readonly窄Pick BootProbeRuntime實際methods、AudioSession generation/frontier/clock/readPCM/readEvidence/alignment、MicRuntime controlConnected/publisher/sampleRate、BackingRuntime connected/isRobot/sampleRate、SourceRuntime connected/socket、CalibrationSession collecting/result/transactionActive/failPreservingPrimed/applyExternalResult、TimingRuntime calibrationKind/beginBootProbe/restoreCandidateKindToAuthority/markBootProbeAuthority。query ports明列robotRouteActive/robotProbeTimingActive/takeBlocksCalibration/micPlayable/backingPlayable/calibrationIsStale/probeStatus/appliedKind/applicability/roomHasSong/deltaFresh/currentDelta/currentPlaybackRate/bootAdvance；原無參數queries不強迫共用now。具名sendProbe/reportTimingStatus與原debug log位置，常數明列sampleRate/lead/searchMargin/reference/analysisTimeout/minCorrelation/maxCaptureGap/reapplyThreshold/debug。不要傳整個config/caller mutable bag、任意setter或optional假production port。factory constructor inert，在所有owners建構後初始化，原hoisted callbacks只在後續事件使用；若發現constructor立即回調，停止重畫依賴，不用let!。
- identity表：requestId在BootProbeRuntime.nextRequestId產生，abandon不重設；target/request sessionGeneration/capture generation在原send時點產生；serverSentAtMs沿原tick monotonicnow。Mic client reply先由ProbeLifecycle驗Number(requestId)及uint32generation，錯generation不consume；Backing reply不新增capture echo要求。consume後共同fence先active/sessiongeneration，session不current不讀livecapture；然後target capturegeneration。identity錯abandon→timingpub、不fail/spend attempt。pendingAnalysis同fence先於timeout/wait/DSP。completed/MicLeg context只session/Mic/Backinggens＋兩rates、不含Source mappinggen；同rate不能代替capture identity。
